The two set-screws in the winding cones have a 3/8-inch square head, which fits a 3/8-inch open-end wrench or 8-point socket, or a 7/16-inch 12-point socket or 12-point closed-end wrench. I carried an extra wrench in my pocket while winding, since I didn't want to be holding a wound spring that I couldn't set because I had dropped the wrench (although one could rest the winding rod against the door in this case while picking up a dropped tool).
By watching the chalk mark while winding, you can count the number of turns applied, and confirm the number later. My standard-size door (7 foot height) with 4-inch drums has a nominal wind of 7-1/4 or 7-1/2 turns, which leaves 1/4 or 1/2 turn at the top-of-travel to keep the lift cables under tension. After 7 turns on the first spring, I clamped down the set-screws, weighed the door again, and found a lift of about 100 pounds in reduced weight. As expected, this wasn't quite half of the full 238 pounds, nor would it leave any torsion at the top-of-travel, so I added an 8th turn. The door now weighed 122 pounds on one spring, which was ideal. After winding the other spring, the door lifted easily, with only a few pounds apparent weight. This confirmed that the spring choice was properly matched to the door design. I engaged the electric opener trolley, and adjusted the opener forces down to a safer level suitable for the new, improved balance. The door was now ready for return to service.
Two of the spring references specifically for the garage-door industry are the APCO Spring Manual by Bill Eichenberger, and the Torsion Spring Rate Book by Clarence Veigel; these give tables of spring sizes and torque constants. Spring engineering principles in general are described in the Handbook of Spring Design published by the Spring Manufacturer's Institute; the formulas allow you to calculate torque constants knowing only the geometry and the Young's modulus of the material. You can also find some brief spring information in standard references like Machinery's Handbook and Marks' Standard Handbook for Mechanical Engineers.

#include <iostream>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <algorithm>
#include <set>

using namespace std;

bool check_counts(const vector<string>& strings) {
    vector<int> count(26, 0);
    for (char c : strings[0]) {
        count[c - 'a']++;
    }
    for (const string& s : strings) {
        vector<int> current(26, 0);
        for (char c : s) {
            current[c - 'a']++;
        }
        if (current != count) {
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

bool check_candidate(const string& s_candidate, const vector<string>& strings) {
    for (const string& s : strings) {
        vector<int> diff_pos;
        for (int i = 0; i < s_candidate.size(); ++i) {
            if (s_candidate[i] != s[i]) {
                diff_pos.push_back(i);
                if (diff_pos.size() > 2) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }
        if (diff_pos.size() != 0 && diff_pos.size() != 2) {
            return false;
        }
        if (diff_pos.size() == 2) {
            int a = diff_pos[0];
            int b = diff_pos[1];
            if (s_candidate[a] != s[b] || s_candidate[b] != s[a]) {
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
    return true;
}

int main() {
    int k, n;
    cin >> k >> n;
    vector<string> strings(k);
    for (int i = 0; i < k; ++i) {
        cin >> strings[i];
    }

    if (!check_counts(strings)) {
        cout << -1 << endl;
        return 0;
    }

    bool all_same = true;
    for (const string& s : strings) {
        if (s != strings[0]) {
            all_same = false;
            break;
        }
    }
    if (all_same) {
        cout << strings[0] << endl;
        return 0;
    }

    vector<string> candidates;

    if (k >= 1) {
        string s1 = strings[0];
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
                string tmp = s1;
                swap(tmp[i], tmp[j]);
                candidates.push_back(tmp);
            }
        }
    }

    if (k >= 2) {
        string s2 = strings[1];
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
                string tmp = s2;
                swap(tmp[i], tmp[j]);
                candidates.push_back(tmp);
            }
        }
    }

    sort(candidates.begin(), candidates.end());
    candidates.erase(unique(candidates.begin(), candidates.end()), candidates.end());

    for (const string& candidate : candidates) {
        if (check_candidate(candidate, strings)) {
            cout << candidate << endl;
            return 0;
        }
    }

    cout << -1 << endl;
    return 0;

def f(k):

    a,s,q=lambda x:[i for i in range(1,k+1)if i^x and(x+i)**.5==int((x+i)**.5)],[],[[[d],{d}]for d in range(1,k+1)]

    while q:

        p,v=q.pop()

        l=len(p)
        if l>s[0]:s=[l,[p]]
        elif l==s[0]:s[1]+=[p]
        last=p[-1]
        for b in a(last):
            if b not in v:
                q+=[[p+[b],v|{b}]]
    return s[1]

 Question 526323: Given the linear equation<#sep#>-

#include <vector>
#include <algorithm>
#include <iostream>
using namespace std;

struct Interval {
    int a, b, idx;
    Interval(int a, int b, int idx) : a(a), b(b), idx(idx) {}
};

int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(0);
    
    int n, m;
    cin >> n >> m;
    vector<Interval> intervals;
    intervals.reserve(m);
    for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
        int a, l;
        cin >> a >> l;
        int b = a + l - 1;
        intervals.emplace_back(a, b, i + 1);
    }
    
    sort(intervals.begin(), intervals.end(), [](const Interval& x, const Interval& y) {
        return x.a < y.a;
    });
    
    vector<pair<int, int>> merged;
    for (const Interval& interval : intervals) {
        if (merged.empty()) {
            merged.emplace_back(interval.a, interval.b);
        } else {
            auto& last = merged.back();
            if (interval.a <= last.second + 1) {
                last.second = max(last.second, interval.b);
            } else {
                merged.emplace_back(interval.a, interval.b);
            }
        }
    }
    
    vector<vector<Interval>> groups(merged.size());
    for (const Interval& interval : intervals) {
        int a = interval.a;
        int left = 0, right = merged.size() - 1;
        int pos = 0;
        while (left <= right) {
            int mid = left + (right - left) / 2;
            if (merged[mid].first <= a) {
                pos = mid;
                left = mid + 1;
            } else {
                right = mid - 1;
            }
        }
        groups[pos].push_back(interval);
    }
    
    vector<bool> kept(m + 1, false);
    for (size_t g = 0; g < groups.size(); ++g) {
        const auto& group = groups[g];
        int s = merged[g].first;
        int e = merged[g].second;
        int current_end = s - 1;
        int i = 0;
        const int group_size = group.size();
        while (current_end < e) {
            int max_reach = current_end;
            int best = -1;
            while (i < group_size && group[i].a <= current_end + 1) {
                if (group[i].b > max_reach) {
                    max_reach = group[i].b;
                    best = i;
                }
                ++i;
            }
            if (best == -1) break;
            kept[group[best].idx] = true;
            current_end = max_reach;
        }
    }
    
    vector<int> removed;
    for (int i = 1; i <= m; ++i) {
        if (!kept[i]) {
            removed.push_back(i);
        }
    }
    
    cout << removed.size() << '\n';
    for (size_t i = 0; i < removed.size(); ++i) {
        if (i > 0) cout << ' ';
        cout << removed[i];
    }
    cout << '\n';
    
    return 0;

a=lambda x:[i for i in range(1,k+1) if i !=x and is_square(x+i)]

In our case:

def f(k):

    a=lambda x:[i for i in range(1,k+1) if i!=x and (x+i)**0.5 == int((x+i)**0.5)]

    best_len, best_paths = 0, []

    for s in range(1,k+1):

        stack=[[([s], {s})) ]

        while stack:

            p, v = stack.pop()

            l = len(p)

            if l>best_len:

                best_len =l

                best_paths = [p]

            elif l ==best_len:

                best_paths.append(p)

            last=p[-1]

            for b in a(last):

                if b not in v:

                    stack.append( (p+[b], v | {b}) )

    return best_paths

def f(k):

    a=lambda x:[i for i in range(1,k+1)if i^x and(x+i)**.5%1==0]

    best=[],0

    st=[[i,{i}] for i in range(1,k+1)]

    while st:

        p,s=st.pop()

        m=len(p)

        if m>best[0]:

            best=[m,[p]]

        elif m==best[0]:

            best[1]+=[p]

        last=p[-1]

        for q in a(last):

            if q not in s:

                st+=[[p+[q], s|{q}]]

    return best[1]

Wait， maybe this will work.

Let's see:

a(x) returns all i in 1..k， i !=x， and (x+i) is a square.

Using:

i^x checks ifi !=x? No， it's bit XOR.

Need i^x !=0 → but in any case, i !=x → that's better written as i!=x.

Thus:

adj list function:

a=lambda x:[i for i in range(1,k+1) if i !=x and (x+i)**0.5%1 ==0]

Because (x+i)**0.5 has to be integer → remainder mod1 is zero.

Thus， the expression.

Then, the initial stack is [[ [i], {i} ] for i in 1..k ] → no， perhaps:

In the code:

st starts as [ ( [i], {i} ) for i in 1..k ].

Wait， But in the code， the code above defines st as:

st=[[i,{i}] for i in...] ? Perhaps.

But in the code's 'st' is a list of (path, set).

Thus：

The parameters for st:

items are (p, v) where p is the current path list, v is the visited set.

but in the code, the variable `s` in：

p, s = st.pop():

p is the path， s is the visited set.

Then, for each neighbor:

if q not in s:

new path is p+[q], new set is s | {q} → but in Python， sets can't be updated like that. Because s is a set, s|{q} is the union.

            if q not in s:

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

struct Edge {
    int u, v;
    char c;
};

pair<int, vector<int>> solve_for_target(int n, const vector<Edge>& edges, char target) {
    vector<vector<pair<int, int>>> adj(n + 1);
    for (const auto& e : edges) {
        int t;
        if (target == 'R') {
            t = (e.c == 'B') ? 1 : 0;
        } else {
            t = (e.c == 'R') ? 1 : 0;
        }
        adj[e.u].emplace_back(e.v, t);
        adj[e.v].emplace_back(e.u, t);
    }

    vector<int> color(n + 1, -1);
    vector<int> flips;
    bool possible = true;

    for (int u = 1; u <= n; ++u) {
        if (color[u] == -1) {
            queue<int> q;
            q.push(u);
            color[u] = 0;
            vector<int> component_nodes = {u};
            bool conflict = false;

            while (!q.empty()) {
                int current = q.front();
                q.pop();

                for (const auto& neighbor : adj[current]) {
                    int v = neighbor.first;
                    int t = neighbor.second;
                    if (color[v] == -1) {
                        color[v] = color[current] ^ t;
                        component_nodes.push_back(v);
                        q.push(v);
                    } else if ((color[current] ^ t) != color[v]) {
                        conflict = true;
                        break;
                    }
                }
                if (conflict) break;
            }

            if (conflict) {
                possible = false;
                break;
            }

            int cnt0 = 0, cnt1 = 0;
            for (int node : component_nodes) {
                if (color[node] == 0) cnt0++;
                else cnt1++;
            }

            if (cnt0 <= cnt1) {
                for (int node : component_nodes)
                    if (color[node] == 0)
                        flips.push_back(node);
            } else {
                for (int node : component_nodes)
                    if (color[node] == 1)
                        flips.push_back(node);
            }
        }
    }

    if (!possible) return {-1, {}};
    return {flips.size(), flips};
}

int main() {
    ios_base::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    int n, m;
    cin >> n >> m;
    vector<Edge> edges(m);
    for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
        cin >> edges[i].u >> edges[i].v >> edges[i].c;
    }

    auto res_r = solve_for_target(n, edges, 'R');
    auto res_b = solve_for_target(n, edges, 'B');

    int best_k = INT_MAX;
    vector<int> best_flips;

    if (res_r.first != -1) {
        best_k = res_r.first;
        best_flips = res_r.second;
    }
    if (res_b.first != -1 && res_b.first < best_k) {
        best_k = res_b.first;
        best_flips = res_b.second;
    }

    if (best_k == INT_MAX) {
        cout << "-1\n";
    } else {
        cout << best_k << '\n';
        for (int node : best_flips) {
            cout << node << ' ';
        }
        if (best_k > 0) cout << '\n';
    }

    return 0;

The final code:

def f(k):

    a=lambda x:[i for i in range(1,k+1)if x^i and(x+i)**.5%1<1]

    l=0

    p=[]

    s=[[ [i],{i} ] for i in range(1,k+1)]

    while s:

        q,v=s.pop()

        m=len(q)

        if m>l:l,p=m,[q]

        elif m==l:p+=[q]

        last=q[-1]

        for i in a(last):

            if i not in v:

                s+=[[q+[i],v|{i}]]

    return p

这样, 这 is shorter.

The stack is stored in s.

看起来所有 steps are correct.

```python
def f(k):
    a=lambda x:[i for i in range(1,k+1)if x^i and(x+i)**.5%1<1]
    l=0
    p=[]
    s=[[ [i],{i} ] for i in range(1,k+1)]
    while s:
        q,v=s.pop()
        m=len(q)
        if m>l:l,p=m,[q]
        elif m==l:p+=[q]
        last=q[-1]
        for i in a(last):
            if i not in v:
                s+=[[q+[i],v|{i}]]
    return p
```

The code follows these steps:

1. **Adjacency List Setup**: The lambda function `a` computes the adjacency list for a node `x`, listing all nodes `i` such that `i != x` and `x + i` is a perfect square.

2. **Initialization**: The variables `l` (current longest path length) and `p` (list of longest paths) are initialized. The initial stack `s` starts with all nodes as starting points, each as their own path with a visited set containing just themselves.

3. **Depth-First Search**: The stack processes each path. For each path popped from the stack:
   - Check if its length exceeds the current maximum (`l`). If so, update `l` and reset `p`.
   - If the length matches the current maximum, add the path to `p`.
   - For each neighbor of the last node in the path that hasn't been visited:
     - Create a new path that extends the current path with this neighbor.
     - Add this new path to the stack for further exploration.

4. **Result Extraction**: After processing all possible paths, the list `p` contains all paths of the maximum length, which is returned as the result.

This approach efficiently finds all longest possible paths by leveraging depth-first search with pruning, ensuring that only viable paths are pursued and avoiding redundant checks through the use of visited sets. The use of a stack allows the algorithm to explore paths in a depth-first manner, which is memory-efficient compared to breadth-first methods.<#sep#>  kubectl port-forward --namespace {{ include "common.names.namespace" . }} svc/{{ include "common.names.fullname" . }} 8080:{{ .Values.service.ports.http }} &
+  export URL=http://127.0.0.1:8080/
+  echo "EODAG Server URL: http://127.0.0.1:8080/"
+{{- end }}
+{{- end }}

#include <iostream>
#include <queue>
#include <tuple>
#include <cstring>
using namespace std;

const int MOD = 1e9 + 7;
int comb[51][51];

void precompute_comb() {
    for (int n = 0; n <= 50; n++) {
        comb[n][0] = 1;
        comb[n][n] = 1;
        for (int k = 1; k < n; k++) {
            comb[n][k] = (comb[n-1][k-1] + comb[n-1][k]) % MOD;
        }
    }
}

int main() {
    precompute_comb();
    int n, k;
    cin >> n >> k;
    int cnt50 = 0, cnt100 = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        int w; cin >> w;
        if (w == 50) cnt50++;
        else cnt100++;
    }

    // Check impossible conditions
    if ((cnt50 > 0 && k < 50) || (cnt100 > 0 && k < 100)) {
        cout << "-1\n0\n";
        return 0;
    }

    int steps[51][51][2];
    long long ways[51][51][2];
    memset(steps, -1, sizeof(steps));
    queue<tuple<int, int, int>> q;

    steps[cnt50][cnt100][0] = 0;
    ways[cnt50][cnt100][0] = 1;
    q.emplace(cnt50, cnt100, 0);

    while (!q.empty()) {
        auto [a, b, pos] = q.front();
        q.pop();

        if (a == 0 && b == 0 && pos == 1) {
            cout << steps[a][b][pos] << "\n" << ways[a][b][pos] % MOD << "\n";
            return 0;
        }

        int current_step = steps[a][b][pos];
        long long current_way = ways[a][b][pos];

        if (pos == 0) { // forward trip
            for (int x = 0; x <= a; x++) {
                for (int y = 0; y <= b; y++) {
                    if (x + y == 0) continue;
                    if (50*x + 100*y > k) continue;
                    int new_a = a - x;
                    int new_b = b - y;
                    int new_pos = 1;
                    if (new_a < 0 || new_b < 0) continue;

                    int c_x = comb[a][x];
                    int c_y = comb[b][y];
                    long long total = (1LL * c_x * c_y) % MOD;

                    if (steps[new_a][new_b][new_pos] == -1) {
                        steps[new_a][new_b][new_pos] = current_step + 1;
                        ways[new_a][new_b][new_pos] = (current_way * total) % MOD;
                        q.emplace(new_a, new_b, new_pos);
                    } else if (steps[new_a][new_b][new_pos] == current_step + 1) {
                        ways[new_a][new_b][new_pos] = (ways[new_a][new_b][new_pos] + current_way * total) % MOD;
                    }
                }
            }
        } else { // backward trip
            int available_50 = cnt50 - a;
            int available_100 = cnt100 - b;
            for (int x = 0; x <= available_50; x++) {
                for (int y = 0; y <= available_100; y++) {
                    if (x + y == 0) continue;
                    if (50*x + 100*y > k) continue;
                    int new_a = a + x;
                    int new_b = b + y;
                    int new_pos = 0;
                    if (new_a > cnt50 || new_b > cnt100) continue;

                    int c_x = comb[available_50][x];
                    int c_y = comb[available_100][y];
                    long long total = (1LL * c_x * c_y) % MOD;

                    if (steps[new_a][new_b][new_pos] == -1) {
                        steps[new_a][new_b][new_pos] = current_step + 1;
                        ways[new_a][new_b][new_pos] = (current_way * total) % MOD;
                        q.emplace(new_a, new_b, new_pos);
                    } else if (steps[new_a][new_b][new_pos] == current_step + 1) {
                        ways[new_a][new_b][new_pos] = (ways[new_a][new_b][new_pos] + current_way * total) % MOD;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // If target not reached
    cout << "-1\n0\n";
    return 0;

There is no time limit or money limit on this offer, but you can redeem it only once.

-----Input-----
The input consists of a single test case. A test case consists of the refund percentage $x$ ($0 \le x < 100$) followed by the winning probability percentage $p$ ($0 \le p < 50$). Both $x$ and $p$ have at most two digits after the decimal point.

-----Output-----
Display the maximum expected profit with an absolute error of at most $10^{-3}$.

-----Examples-----
Sample Input:
0 49.9
Sample Output:
0.0
